4.2
Product and sum rules

The evaluation functors
ev_{A}\colon{\bf Set}^{{\bf A}^{op}\times{\bf B}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
jointly create limits and colimits, and as the
composites

are
\Delta_{A}[F](\Phi)
,
the limit rules of Section
3.2
lift to

\Delta{[}F{]}
.

Theorem
3.1
gives the following.

Theorem 4.1
.

(1)

If
\Gamma\colon{\bf I}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\mathcal{}T}\!\!{\it ense}({\bf Set}^{\bf A},{\bf Set}^{\bf B})
then

\Delta[\varinjlim_{I}\Gamma I]\cong\varinjlim_{I}\Delta[\Gamma I]
.

(2)

If
{\bf I}
is non-empty and connected and

\Gamma\colon{\bf I}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\mathcal{}T}\!\!{\it ense}({\bf Set}^{\bf A},{\bf Set}^{\bf B})
,
then
\Delta[\varprojlim_{I}\Gamma I]\cong\varprojlim_{I}\Delta[\Gamma I]
.

(3)

For any set
I
and tense functors
F_{i}
,

i\in I
, we have

\Delta\Bigl[\prod_{i\in I}F_{i}\Bigr]\cong\sum_{J\subsetneqq I}\Bigl(\prod_{j\in J}F_{j}\Bigr)\times\prod_{k\notin J}\Delta[F_{k}].

Corollary 4.1
.

(1)
\Delta[F+G]\cong\Delta{[}F{]}+\Delta[G]
.

(2)
\Delta[C\cdot F]\cong C\cdot\Delta{[}F{]}
for any set
C
.

(3)
\Delta[F\times G]\cong(\Delta{[}F{]}\times G)+(F\times\Delta[G])+(\Delta{[}F{]}\times\Delta[G])
.

Note that on the right hand side of (3) we have

\Delta{[}F{]}\times G
for example.
\Delta{[}F{]}
is a functor

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{{\bf A}^{op}\times{\bf B}}
whereas
G
is a functor

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
. Looking at where

Proposition 4.3
.

For a profunctor
P\colon{\bf A}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}{\bf B}

\Delta[P\otimes(\ \ )](\Phi)\cong P\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}

This is just a restatement of Proposition

We think of

as a linear functor with coefficients

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{{\bf A}^{op}\times{\bf B}}

with constant value

.

Corollary 4.2
.

\Delta[\id_{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}](\Phi)=\Id_{\bf A}

where
\id_{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf A}
is the identity functor and

\Id_{\bf A}\colon{\bf A}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}{\bf A}
is the
identity profunctor.

Like we did in Proposition
3.4
, we can
generalize
4.3
to the following:

Proposition 4.4
.

If
F\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}

preserves binary coproducts, then

\Delta{[}F{]}(A,B)=F({\bf A}(A,-))(B)

i.e.
\Delta{[}F{]}=C\!or(F)
, the core of
F

(see Definition
1.2
).

We can improve (2) in Corollary
4.1
,
replacing the set
C
by a profunctor
P\colon{\bf B}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}{\bf C}
. Given a tense functor
F\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
, we can compose it
with
P\otimes(\ \ )
to get another tense functor

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.47023pt\raise 5.39168pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.39168pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{F}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 7.5442pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.08841pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.70618pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{P\otimes(\ \ )}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 20.13728pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 46.27457pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf C}

which will be called
P\otimes F
as its value at
\Phi

is
P\otimes(F(\Phi))
although it might be hard to parse.

Proposition 4.5
.

\Delta[P\otimes F]\cong P\otimes\Delta{[}F{]}\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}

Proof.

The
P\otimes F
is the composite

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.47023pt\raise 5.39168pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.39168pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{F}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 7.5442pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.08841pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.70618pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{P\otimes(\ \ )}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 20.13728pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 46.27457pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf C}

so
\Delta[P\otimes F]
is the functor
{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{{\bf A}^{op}\times{\bf C}}
with values

\Delta[P\otimes F](\Phi)(A,C)=\Delta_{A}[P\otimes F](\Phi)(C)\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}

On the other hand
P\otimes\Delta{[}F{]}
is the composite

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.46855pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\Delta{[}F{]}}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 13.09116pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 32.18231pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{{\bf A}^{op}\times{\bf B}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.56291pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{P\otimes_{\bf B}(\ \ )}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.88632pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 49.77264pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{{\bf A}^{op}\times{\bf C}}

so has values

(P\otimes\Delta{[}F{]})(\Phi)(A,C)=(P\otimes_{\bf B}(\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)))(A,C)\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}

By the definition of composition of profunctors, this is

\begin{array}[]{lll}&&\int^{B}P(B,C)\times\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,B)\\
&=&\int^{B}P(B,C)\times\Delta_{A}[F](\Phi)(B)\\
&=&(P\otimes\Delta_{A}[F](\Phi))(C)\end{array}

and by Proposition
3.5
this is isomorphic to

\Delta_{A}[P\otimes F](\Phi)(C)
.
∎

4.3
A natural reformulation

It will be conceptually clearer to reformulate the definition of

\Delta
in more categorical terms, that is, in terms of
natural transformations, Yoneda style. This rids us of many of the
element-based proofs, eliminating, as it does, membership
and especially non-membership. The results are cleaner and
clearer, especially in the next section where we see the
chain rule reduced to composition. This is a vast improvement
over the construction and proof of the one-variable chain rule
given in
(
Paré,, 2024
)
which is far from transparent.

So why not just start with this as a definition? The basic
intuition of finite differences would be lost. It is hard to imagine
why one would define a profunctor using (2) or (3) in
the proposition below, or formulate the product and sum
rules or the chain rule.

Proposition 4.6
.

Let
F\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
be a tense
functor and
\Phi
an object of
{\bf Set}^{\bf A}
. Then there
is a natural bijection between the following:

(1) Elements
x\in\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,B)

(2) Natural transformations
t\colon{\bf B}(B,-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))
giving a pullback

\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}0\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \ }
\textstyle{\ {\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}0\ }
\textstyle{\ {\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \ }
Pb

(3) Natural transformations
u\colon F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))
giving a pullback

\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
Pb

Proof.

An element of
\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,B)
is an element of

F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))(B)
which is not in
F(\Phi)(B)
.
By Yoneda, this corresponds bijectively to a natural
transformation

t\colon{\bf B}(B,-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))

for which
t(B)(1_{B})\notin F(\Phi)(B)
. As
F(\Phi)\ \>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@hook{1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))
is complemented by tenseness,
that’s equivalent to none of the values of
t
being in

F(\Phi)
, which means that

\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}0\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \ }
\textstyle{\ {\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}0\ }
\textstyle{\ {\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\scriptstyle{t}
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \ }

is a pullback. And this, in turn, is equivalent to

\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)\ }
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\scriptstyle{u}
\textstyle{\ F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\ }
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)\ \hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}

being a pullback, where
u
is the inclusion on the first
summand and
t
on the second.
∎

As mentioned in
1.1
it is useful to think of the elements of
a profunctor as some sort of morphism but between objects of
different categories (sometimes called heteromorphisms).
Because of the representables appearing in the natural
transformations above, it’s not unreasonable to think of them
as morphisms from
A
to
B
, as a kind of Kleisli
morphism although
F
is not a monad. If
F
were the
identity for example,
t
is equivalent to a natural
transformation
{\bf B}(B,-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf A}(A,-)
so to an
actual morphism
A\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>B

As

,

\textstyle{\Delta{[}\Phi_{0}{]}(A^{\prime},B^{\prime})}
\scriptstyle{\Delta{[}\Phi_{0}{]}(f,g)}

is a pullback.

An element of
\Delta[\Phi](A,B)

4.4
Lax chain rule

We saw in
(
Paré,, 2024
)
that the chain rule for the single
variable functorial difference was expressed as a laxity
morphism rather than an isomorphism, and the same applies
in the multivariable case. For tense functors

F\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
and

G\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf C}
we will
construct a comparison transformation

\gamma(\Phi)\colon\Delta[G](F(\Phi))\otimes_{\bf B}\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\Delta[GF](\Phi)

and establish associativity and unit laws for it. In fact,
considering
\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)
as a profunctor may not
mean much unless it composes like a profunctor.
Otherwise it is just an object of
{\bf Set}^{{\bf A}^{op}\times{\bf B}}
.

The construction of
\gamma
in
(
Paré,, 2024
)
is perhaps
a bit opaque and the profunctor interpretation clarifies
this. We’ll see that it is, in a sense, just composition as it
should be.

In the previous section we described the functoriality of

\Delta
in terms of the characterization (2) of
Proposition
4.6
, but for the chain rule
the characterization (3) is better, so we reformulate the
functorialities in this context. As we will refer to it a lot,
let us call a natural transformation
t
such that

\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)}
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)}
\textstyle{F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)}
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)}
\textstyle{F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)}
\textstyle{F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\scriptstyle{t}
\textstyle{F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\textstyle{\mbox{\rule[-7.11317pt]{0.0pt}{19.91692pt}}F(\Phi)}

is a pullback, a PPI transformation (for pullback preserves
injections).

Functoriality of
\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,B)
, considered as a set
of PPI transformations, is easy. It’s just composition with

F(\Phi+{\bf A}(f,-))
and
F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(g,-)

respectively.

Functoriality in
\Phi
and
F
are a
bit
more
complicated as the
\Phi
and
F
appear in both the
domain and codomain of
t
. The following characterization
will be useful, although it is nothing but a reformulation.

Proposition 4.7
.

Let

Theorem 4.2
.

For tense functors
F\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
and

G\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf C}
there is a natural
transformation

\gamma\colon(\Delta[G]\circ F)\otimes\Delta{[}F{]}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\Delta[GF]

which is:

(1)

and
G

(2)

(3)

G
, let
\beta\colon G\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>G^{\prime}
be a
tense natural transformation. We’ll show that

\textstyle{\Delta{[}G^{\prime}{]}(F(\Phi))\otimes_{\bf B}\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}
\textstyle{\Delta{[}G^{\prime}F{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}
\scriptstyle{\gamma(\Phi)}
\textstyle{\Delta{[}G{]}(F(\Phi))\otimes_{\bf B}\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}
\textstyle{\Delta{[}G^{\prime}{]}(F(\Phi))\otimes_{\bf B}\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}
\textstyle{\Delta{[}G{]}(F(\Phi))\otimes_{\bf B}\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}
\textstyle{\Delta{[}GF{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}
\scriptstyle{\gamma(\Phi)}
\textstyle{\Delta{[}GF{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}
\textstyle{\Delta{[}G^{\prime}F{]}(\Phi)(A,C)}

commutes.
An element
u\otimes t
of the domain, goes down to

u^{\prime}\otimes t
and then
G^{\prime}u^{\prime}\cdot t
for
u^{\prime}
such that

\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\scriptstyle{u^{\prime}}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\scriptstyle{\beta F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{G(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))}
\scriptstyle{u}
\textstyle{G(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\scriptstyle{\beta(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))}

u\otimes t
goes across to
Gt\cdot u
and then down
to
v^{\prime}
such that

\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\scriptstyle{v^{\prime}}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\scriptstyle{\beta F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\scriptstyle{Gt\cdot u}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\scriptstyle{\beta(F(\Phi)+{\bf A}(A,-))}

If we paste the diagram for
u^{\prime}
with the naturality square

\textstyle{G^{\prime}(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\scriptstyle{G^{\prime}t}
\textstyle{G(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))}
\scriptstyle{\beta(F\Phi+{\bf B}(B,-))}
\textstyle{G(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\scriptstyle{Gt}
\textstyle{GF(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\textstyle{G^{\prime}F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}
\scriptstyle{\beta F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))}

and compare with the diagram for
v^{\prime}
we see that

v^{\prime}=G^{\prime}t\cdot u^{\prime}
, which gives naturality in
G
.

Let

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.47023pt\raise 5.39168pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.39168pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{F}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 7.5442pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.08841pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48119pt\raise 7.99396pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98792pt\raise be tense functors. Associativity involves taking an element

v\otimes u\otimes t
of

\Delta[H](GF(\Phi))\otimes_{\bf B}\Delta[G](F\Phi)\otimes_{\bf C}\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)

at
(A,D)
and applying
\gamma
in two different
ways to reduce it to elements of
\Delta[HGF](\Phi)
,
and seeing that they are equal. This is for any PPI
transformations

\displaystyle t\colon F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-)

\displaystyle\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))

\displaystyle u\colon GF(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)

\displaystyle\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>G(F(\Phi)+{\bf B}(B,-))

\displaystyle v\colon HGF(\Phi)+{\bf D}(D,-)

\displaystyle\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>H(GF(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-))\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}

And indeed, we get

\textstyle{(Hu\cdot v)\otimes t}
\textstyle{HGt\cdot Hu\cdot v\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\textstyle{v\otimes u\otimes t}
\textstyle{(Hu\cdot v)\otimes t}
\textstyle{v\otimes u\otimes t}
\textstyle{v\otimes(Gt\cdot u)}
\textstyle{v\otimes(Gt\cdot u)}
\textstyle{HGt\cdot Hu\cdot v\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}}
\textstyle{v\otimes(Gt\cdot u)}
\textstyle{H(Gt\cdot u)\cdot v}

For the unit laws, first assume that
{\bf B}={\bf A}

and that
F=\id_{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
. Then
\gamma(\Phi)

takes the form

\gamma(\Phi)\colon\Delta[G]\otimes_{\bf A}\Delta[\id_{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}](\Phi)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\Delta[G](\Phi)

and an element of the domain is an equivalence class

u\otimes t
for PPI’s

\Phi+{\bf A}(A^{\prime},-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48463pt\raise 5.15279pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.15279pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{t}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.99506pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 17.99011pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-)\quad G(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48056pt\raise 4.50694pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{u}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.84459pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 19.68918pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>G(\Phi+{\bf A}(A^{\prime},-))\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}

For
t
to be a PPI it must be of the form

\Phi+{\bf A}(A^{\prime},-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.54385pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\Phi+{\bf A}(f,-)}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 22.58594pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 51.17188pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-)

and every equivalence class has a unique representative
where
f
is
1_{A}
. Then
\gamma(\Phi)(u\otimes 1)=u

gives our bijective right unitor.

For the left unitor, let
{\bf B}={\bf C}
and
G=\id_{{\bf Set}^{\bf C}}
.
Then
\gamma
takes the form

\gamma(\Phi)\colon\Delta[\id_{{\bf Set}^{\bf C}}](F(\Phi)\otimes\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi))\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)

and an element of its domain is an equivalence class

u\otimes t
with PPI’s

F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C^{\prime},-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48463pt\raise 5.15279pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.15279pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{t}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.99506pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 17.99011pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi+{\bf A}(A,-))\quad F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C,-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48056pt\raise 4.50694pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{u}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.84459pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 19.68918pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(C^{\prime},-)

For
u
to be a PPI it must be of the form
F(\Phi)+{\bf C}(g,-)
. Again every equivalence class contains a
unique representative with
g=1_{C}
. Then

\gamma(\Phi)(1\otimes t)=t

gives the bijective unitor.
∎

As stated, the lax chain rule is called lax just because what
might have been hoped to be an isomorphism is merely a
comparison morphism reducing a more complicated expression
to a simpler one. But, if we reformulate it in terms of the
tangent bundle of Section
4.1
, we get
an actual lax normal functor.

Recall that the tangent functor
T[F]

\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\scriptstyle{F}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\times{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\scriptstyle{P_{1}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\times{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\times{\bf Set}^{\bf B}}
\scriptstyle{T{[}F{]}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\times{\bf Set}^{\bf B}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\scriptstyle{P_{1}}

is given by

T[F](\Phi,\Psi)=(F(\Phi),\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)\otimes_{\bf A}\Psi)\hbox to0.0pt{\,.\hss}

If
G\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf C}
is another
tense functor, then the composite

T[G]\circ T[F]=(GF(\Phi),\Delta[G](F(\Phi))\otimes_{\bf B}\Delta{[}F{]}(\Phi)\otimes_{\bf A}\Phi)

and

(1_{GF(\Phi)},\gamma(\Phi)\otimes_{\bf A}\Psi)\colon T[G]\circ T[F]\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>T[GF]

makes
T\colon{\mathcal{}T}\!\!{\it ense}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\mathcal{}T}\!\!{\it ense}
into a lax normal
functor. We omit the details which only involve the
rearrangement of the facts proved in Theorem
4.2
.

5
Newton series

5.1
Multivariable Newton series

The Newton series of a function of a real variable

f\colon{\mathbb{R}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\mathbb{R}}
is a discrete version
of Taylor series. Its aim is to recover
f
from its iterated
differences, or to approximate
f
by polynomials. The
formula is well-known

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Delta^{n}{[}f{]}(0)}{n!}x^{\downarrow n}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Delta^{n}{[}f{]}(0)\binom{x}{n}

when
x^{\downarrow n}
is the falling power

x(x-1)\dots(x-n+1)
and
\binom{x}{n}

is the “binomial coefficient”

\frac{x(x-1)\dots(x-n+1)}{n!}
.

Although not so well-known, a recursive argument
produces a multivariable version: for
f\colon{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\mathbb{R}}
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}=0}^{\infty}\frac{\Delta^{k_{1}}_{x_{1}}\Delta^{k_{2}}_{x_{2}}\cdots\Delta^{k_{n}}_{x_{n}}{[}f{]}(0,\ldots,0)}{k_{1}!k_{2}!\cdots k_{n}!}x_{1}^{\downarrow k_{1}}x_{2}^{\downarrow k_{2}}\cdots x_{n}^{\downarrow k_{n}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1},k_{2},\ldots,k_{n}=0}^{\infty}\Delta^{k_{1}}_{x_{1}}\Delta^{k_{2}}_{x_{2}}\cdots\Delta^{k_{n}}_{x_{n}}{[}f{]}(0,\ldots,0)\binom{x_{1}}{k_{1}}\binom{x_{2}}{k_{2}}\cdots\binom{x_{n}}{k_{n}}\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

In
(
Paré,, 2024
)
we gave a categorified version for taut
endofunctors of
{\bf Set}
and showed that for analytic
functors their Newton series converge to them. In fact
this holds for a larger class of taut functors, which we
call soft analytic. Not only that, the approximation alluded
to above manifests itself as a categorical adjointness.
In this section we develop multivariable versions of these
results.

5.2
Soft multivariable analytic functors

In order to categorify multivariable Newton series we
must modify the notion of
{\bf A}
-
{\bf B}
symmetric
sequence to take into account the extra structure that
the iterated differences have. We replace the category

!{\bf A}
of
(
Fiore et al.,, 2008
)
by the larger category
{\downarrow}{\bf A}

with the same objects, finite sequences
\langle A_{1},\dots,A_{n}\rangle

of objects of
{\bf A}
, but where the morphisms

\langle A_{1},\dots,A_{n}\rangle\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\langle C_{1},\dots C_{m}\rangle

are pairs
(\sigma,\langle f_{j}\rangle)
such that
\sigma\colon m\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>n
is a
surjection
and
\langle f_{j}\rangle
is a family
of morphisms indexed by
m

f_{j}\colon A_{\sigma j}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>C_{j}\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

Composition is formally the same as for
!{\bf A}

(\tau,\langle g_{k}\rangle)(\sigma,\langle f_{j}\rangle)=(\sigma\tau,\langle g_{k}f_{\tau k}\rangle)\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

Whereas
!{\bf A}
is the free symmetric strict monoidal
category generated by
{\bf A}
,
{\downarrow}{\bf A}
is the
free symmetric monoidal category in which every object
has a canonical cocommutative coassociative
comultiplication.

Definition 5.1
.

A
soft

{\bf A}
-
{\bf B}
-
symmetric sequence

is a profunctor
P\colon{\downarrow}{\bf A}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}{\bf B}
.

Given a soft
{\bf A}
-
{\bf B}
-symmetric sequence

P\colon{\downarrow}{\bf A}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}{\bf B}
we define
the functor
\widetilde{P}\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
by the formula

\widetilde{P}(\Phi)(B)=\int^{\langle A_{1}\dots A_{n}\rangle\in{\downarrow}{\bf A}}P(A_{1},\dots,A_{n};B)\times\Phi A_{1}\times{\dots}\times\Phi A_{n}\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

Of course, for this to make sense
\Phi A_{1}\times{\dots}\times\Phi A_{n}
must come from a functor
{\downarrow}{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}
,
which is indeed the case. For a morphism

(\sigma,\langle f_{1}\dots f_{m}\rangle)\colon\langle A_{1},\dots,A_{n}\rangle\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\langle C_{1},\dots,C_{m}\rangle

we have a unique morphism making

\textstyle{\Phi A_{\sigma j}}
\textstyle{\Phi C_{j}}
\scriptstyle{\Phi f_{j}}
\textstyle{\Phi A_{1}\times{\dots}\times\Phi A_{n}}
\textstyle{\Phi A_{\sigma j}}
\scriptstyle{{\rm proj}_{\sigma j}}
\textstyle{\Phi A_{1}\times{\dots}\times\Phi A_{n}}
\textstyle{\Phi C_{1}\times{\dots}\times\Phi C_{m}}
\textstyle{\Phi C_{1}\times{\dots}\times\Phi C_{m}}
\textstyle{\Phi C_{j}}
\scriptstyle{{\rm proj}_{j}}

commute for all
j\in m
.

A more conceptual description of
\widetilde{P}
is
in terms of Kan extensions. Let
Q\colon({\downarrow}{\bf A})^{op}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf A}
be the functor defined by

Q\langle A_{1}{\dots}A_{n}\rangle={\bf A}(A_{1},-)+{\dots}+{\bf A}(A_{n},-)\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

It is indeed a functor, its value on a morphism

(\sigma,\langle f_{1},{\dots},f_{m}\rangle)\colon\langle A_{1},{\dots},A_{n}\rangle\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\langle C_{1},{\dots},C_{m}\rangle

being the unique morphism making all the squares

\textstyle{{\bf A}(C_{1},-)+{\dots}+{\bf A}(C_{m},-)}
\textstyle{{\bf A}(A_{1},-)+{\dots}+{\bf A}(A_{n},-)}
\textstyle{{\bf A}(C_{j},-)}
\textstyle{{\bf A}(C_{1},-)+{\dots}+{\bf A}(C_{m},-)}
\scriptstyle{{\rm inj}_{j}}
\textstyle{{\bf A}(C_{j},-)}
\textstyle{{\bf A}(A_{\sigma j},-)}
\scriptstyle{{\bf A}(f_{j},-)}
\textstyle{{\bf A}(A_{\sigma j},-)}
\textstyle{{\bf A}(A_{1},-)+{\dots}+{\bf A}(A_{n},-)}
\scriptstyle{{\rm inj}_{\sigma j}}

commute. A profunctor
P\colon{\downarrow}{\bf A}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}{\bf B}
is a functor
P\colon({\downarrow}{\bf A})^{op}\times{\bf B}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}
which may be alternately
described as a functor
({\downarrow}{\bf A})^{op}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}
(which we denote by the same letter).
Then
\widetilde{P}
is the left Kan extension of

P
along
Q
:

\textstyle{({\downarrow}{\bf A})^{op}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}}
\scriptstyle{P}
\textstyle{({\downarrow}{\bf A})^{op}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\scriptstyle{Q}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf A}}
\textstyle{{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}}
\scriptstyle{{\rm Lan}_{Q}P=\widetilde{P}}
\scriptstyle{\eta}

Indeed,

{\rm Lan}_{Q}P(\Phi)=\int^{A_{1}{\dots}A_{n}}P(A_{1}{\dots}A_{n};-)\times{\bf Set}^{\bf A}(Q\langle A_{1},{\dots},A_{n}\rangle,\Phi)

(see
Mac Lane, (1971)
, p. 236) and
{\bf Set}^{\bf A}(Q\langle A_{1}{\dots}A_{n}\rangle,\Phi)\cong\Phi A_{1}\times{\dots}\times\Phi A_{n}
.

Q
may be considered as a profunctor
{\downarrow}{\bf A}\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}{\bf A}
and we have the following “softening” of
Proposition
2.13
.

Proposition 5.1
.

1.

\widetilde{P}
is the composite
P\otimes(Q\obslash(\ ))

{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 7.24084pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{Q\obslash(\ )}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.18835pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.37671pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\downarrow{\bf A}}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.55554pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.70592pt\raise 6.5pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.75pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{P\otimes(\ )}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 18.08841pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 42.17682pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

2.

Q
satisfies the condition of 2.4.1.

Proof.

(1) Same as in
2.13
.

(2) Again
\pi_{0}Q(A_{1},{\dots},A_{n};-)=n
for the
same reason (sum of
n
representables), but now for
a morphism
(\sigma,\langle f_{1},{\dots},f_{m}\rangle)\colon\langle A_{1},{\dots},A_{n}\rangle\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\langle C_{1},{\dots},C_{m}\rangle

the morphism

\pi_{0}Q(C_{1},{\dots},C_{m};-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\pi_{0}Q(A_{1},{\dots},A_{n};-)

is

\sigma\colon m\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>n
,
which is onto.
∎

Corollary 5.1
.

\widetilde{P}
is tense.

A more elementary understanding of
\widetilde{P}

will be useful. From the coend formula for Kan extension
we see that an element of
\widetilde{P}(\Phi)(B)

is an equivalence class of pairs
(p,\phi)

\big[p\colon\langle A_{1},{\dots},A_{n}\rangle\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{{}\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 12.78978pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.5pt\raise-1.62846pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\scriptstyle\hskip-2.84526pt\bullet\hskip-2.84526pt}$}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 21.98627pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces}}}}B,\phi\colon\sum{\bf A}(A_{i},-)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>\Phi\big]

where
p\in P(A_{1},{\dots},A_{n};B)
and

\sum{\bf A}(A_{i},-)
is short for
\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf A}(A_{i},-)
. The equivalence relation is generated
by identifying
(p,\phi)
and
(q,\psi)
when there
is a morphism

in
{\downarrow}{\bf A}

such that

\textstyle{B}
\textstyle{\langle C_{1},{\dots},C_{m}\rangle}
\scriptstyle{q}

\textstyle{B}

represents the natural

in

and for

\widetilde{P}(\theta)(B)\colon(p,\phi)\longmapsto(p,\theta\phi)\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

The universal property of Kan extensions says that for any
functor
F\colon{\bf Set}^{\bf A}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>{\bf Set}^{\bf B}

we have a natural bijection

\widetilde{P}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48463pt\raise 5.15279pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.15279pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{t}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 5.99506pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 17.99011pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F\over P\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.48056pt\raise 4.50694pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-1.50694pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{u}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 6.84459pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 19.68918pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>FQ\hbox to0.0pt{\ .\hss}

The correspondence between
t
and
u
is the
following.
t\colon\widetilde{P}\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F
is given by
a family of natural transformations

\langle\widetilde{P}(\Phi)\>\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 4.49588pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{}$}}}\kern 3.0pt}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern 14.99176pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\lx@xy@tip{1}\lx@xy@tip{-1}}}}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}{\hbox{\lx@xy@droprule}}\ignorespaces}}}}\>F(\Phi)\rangle_{\Phi}

, which further breaks

. So for every equivalence
